## Cabo Verde added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cabo Verde’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 books, all of them student (pupil) manuals of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde: *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (Edição 2019), *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (Edição 2020) and *Matemática 7º ano — Manual do(a) aluno(a)* (Edição 2020); 0 teacher guides** — and placed 197 teaching items (Grade 3: 47, Grade 5: 69, Grade 7: 81; 391 PDF pages, every page read). The expansion map’s count of 3 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT. Set boundary, proved from the records: `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly 17 Cabo Verde rows (all Government, grades 1–7), of which exactly three are Mathematics, all student; the download list’s §C enumerates 93 ministry manuals, including Maths manuals for grades 1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 9, 10 and 11 and applied-Maths manuals — every one of those is exact-link-only (no TSV row, no staging file, no GitHub copy) and NOT in hand, so none is cited here; counting any of them would be a set error. Record gap, reported not fixed (record-side): `phase3-push-state.json` contains no Cabo Verde entry at all, and there is no Cabo Verde folder or `books.json` in phase3-staging — the in-hand evidence is the TSV’s GitHub URLs themselves, and all three GitHub copies were located, verified (byte size and MD5; the Grade 5 ministry copy is byte-identical to the GitHub copy, so that book is one book, cited once) and read directly, so the set is settled despite the push-state gap. No duplicates exist in the set: three distinct grades, three distinct books. Printed page = PDF page in all three books. The placements’ named exclusions — front matter, unit openers, objectives lists and the closing national-anthem page in the Grade 5 and Grade 7 books; the Grade 3 book’s cover plate and welcome/mascot pages (PDF pp. 1, 3, 5), its part-opener pages (PDF pp. 55, 85, 107, 111), its closing test “Teste” (printed pp. 125–129 — mixed assessment re-using taught content, no new teaching) and the flag / national-anthem page (PDF p. 130) — and pure exercise / consolidation blocks that teach nothing new are stocked nowhere. The 12 book errors found in the read (2 in the Grade 5 book, 5 in the Grade 7 book, 5 in the Grade 3 book) are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2019; authors Jimmy Leppãnen, Sara Hoye, FvZ; editor Gamlin & Zeipel Communication AB; 140,731,505 bytes, 130 PDF pp., MD5 112ca4f2fc6577b4eb035b0237d9253c (read from the GitHub release copy in release `full-quality-oversized-books`; its byte size equals the ministry HEAD size recorded in the download list §C row 13 exactly). Printed page = PDF page. Pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2020; authors João Duarte, Vanda Delgado; 11,054,236 bytes, 118 PDF pp., MD5 7523a460a8d971a028632077eeb0789c (the GitHub copy and the ministry copy are byte-identical — one book, cited once). Printed page = PDF page throughout the body (its unit-opener pages, PDF pp. 6–7, are unnumbered and are cited by PDF page). Pupil textbook.

Cabo Verde is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above — 4 pupil items in this lesson, from real pupil books of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde. Cabo Verde’s class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Cabo Verde Maths book (all 3) has been read in full and stocked; the link-only ministry Maths manuals for the other grades are not in hand, and Cabo Verde reopens for a grade only if one of those manuals is ever grabbed and read.

## Canada added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Canada's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 works in 4 files: *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (FNESC/FNSA, ©2020), 273 pp. — the one book in Canada's classified in-hand Maths set (0 student + 1 teacher) — and the borderline pupil work *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017), one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book held in three language editions of 11 pp. each and filed in every record under the Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books label, not under Maths** — and placed 117 items (112 teacher-guide evidence items from the guide, items 1–112, plus 5 pupil items from *I Like Math!*, items S1–S5). Every item from the guide is TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE: the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts, with mini-units, activities and blackline masters, and states BC curriculum connections in its tables; it expressly does not explain the mathematics or the curriculum in detail (its printed p. 35) and presumes a trained teacher. No pupil Maths textbook is in hand for Canada: Canada has no national textbook series (education is provincial/territorial), and the five provincial student textbooks in the records (Nelson *Principles of Mathematics 10*; McGraw-Hill Ryerson *Principles of Mathematics 9*; Pearson *Math Makes Sense 3, 7, 8*) stand filled-by-exact-link only — never staged, never secured, not read here and cited nowhere (a sixth, *Nelson Mathematics 8*, is a candidate only, its imprint never read). Canada is therefore cited as EVIDENCE ONLY, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent: Canada is added to this lesson's countries line and named in its rows, but counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The *I Like Math!* items are stocked as pupil evidence from that work exactly as the placements label them — 3 carry taught content (S1, S2, S4) and 2 are context/named-only (S3, S5) — and are likewise counted in no taught-in figure; whether the Nunavut early-books titles count as pupil Maths at all is a record-side question for the parent, reported here, not ruled on. The guide's Water-Keepers items (98–105) rest on the guide's printed teaching alone: the separate FNESC Water Keepers digital resource the guide points to (printed p. 224) is in no record as in hand and was not read. The guide is the 2020 revision of the 2011 *Math First Peoples* resource; the 2011 predecessor located on a Padlet host is not a separate in-hand book and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the guide's introduction, goals/rationale and teacher-reflection material; First Peoples Principles of Learning, story protocols and respectful-practice rules; the resource-design rubric; community-visit apparatus beyond the Interview Guide's six content categories; unit-organization front matter; the cultural/historical context narratives and stories inside the mini-units (context-setting only; no story text is reproduced anywhere in the placements or here); BLM worksheets that merely repeat an itemised activity; formative/summative assessment prompts, guiding questions, video and website lists and the Additional Resources bibliographies; and the *I Like Math!* front/back matter — teach no new mathematics and are stocked nowhere. The 5 book errors found in the read (placements E1–E5) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: E1 (sphere-volume worked example, printed p. 123 — a unit slip and a garbled intermediate line inside a volume calculation; the final answer, 500 cm³, is correct) on item 56; E2 (BLM 6 conversion sheet, printed p. 185 — the percent-to-decimal rule states the decimal-point direction backwards; both printed examples are correct) on item 83; E3 (Moose Tracks answer key, printed p. 181 — mixes “less than or equal to 20” with m < 20 and marks the boundary value inconsistently) on item 71; E4 (Circle Unit Answer Key, printed p. 211 — says “smoke hole” for “fire circle”; the project instructions at printed p. 204 state it correctly) on item 92; E5 (Weaving BLM list, printed p. 55 — numbers both Keep or Toss sheets BLM 16; they are BLM 15 and BLM 16) is a list typo affecting no item's content. Printed page = PDF page in the guide throughout; *I Like Math!* citations are its printed story folios. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (items 2, 39, 46 and S5).

**Book 1:** *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (teacher resource guide) — Canada, First Nations Education Steering Committee (FNESC) and First Nations Schools Association (FNSA), ©2020; 32,264,979 bytes (byte-exact against the record), 273 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts and states curriculum connections; it expressly does not explain the mathematics in detail (printed p. 35); it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *I Like Math!* (pupil book) — Canada, Laura Edlund, illus. Amanda Sandland, Inhabit Education / Government of Nunavut, ©2017 (Inuinnaqtun edition ©2018); one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book in three language editions (English, ISBN 978-1-77266-349-5; Inuktitut, ISBN 978-1-77266-350-1; Inuinnaqtun, ISBN 978-1-77266-977-0), 11 PDF pages each, cited once for the work. Its own book-details page gives its subject as “Daily uses for math”; every record files it under Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books, not Maths. PUPIL EVIDENCE, supplementary — its items are labelled on their rows (taught / context only / named only) and counted in no taught-in figure.

|---|---|

| Measuring facial features in metric units and converting the dimensions to imperial to make a mask that fits | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed p. 104 |

Canada is added to the countries line above and named in this section's rows as EVIDENCE ONLY — teacher-guide evidence from *Math First Peoples* (and, where they land, pupil evidence from *I Like Math!*) — and is NOT added to the acceptance count: no Canada item is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure, on the Ghana evidence-only precedent. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)** — the classified in-hand set is one teacher resource guide with no pupil Maths textbook in hand; the five exact-link provincial student textbooks were not read and are cited nowhere.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 10 items (items 51, 130, 192, 194, 196, 222, 224, 225, 227 and 229).

**Book 1:** *Antología de Matemáticas — Nivel Noveno* (student antología) — Costa Rica, Universidad Estatal a Distancia (UNED) / Colegio Nacional de Educación a Distancia (CONED), 2018; 9,577,886 bytes, 431 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page throughout (the two scanned cover images are PDF pp. 1–2; the title page is printed p. 3). Pupil book (distance-secondary antología).

**Book 2:** *Medidas, 6º — Folleto de prácticas* — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 6.1; 1,476,721 bytes, 26 PDF pp.; a compilation of the practices of the Medidas texto. Pages cited by PDF page. Pupil practice book.

## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items (items 2, 20, 25, 26, 40, 41, 74, 75, 93).

**Book 1:** *Математика за 1 клас* (pupil textbook, Grade 1) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 2nd edition 2026, ISBN 978-619-252-076-2; seven authors headed by Таня Иванова Вълкова; 50,686,679 bytes, 174 PDF pp.; printed folio = PDF page − 1 throughout the body. FULL BOOK (128 lessons, contents to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices). Pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Математика за 2 клас* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 2nd edition 2026, ISBN 978-619-252-077-9; seven authors headed by Таня Иванова Вълкова; 121,741,253 bytes, 146 PDF pp.; printed folio = PDF page − 1. FULL BOOK (112 lessons, contents to printed p. 140). Pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Математика за 3 клас* (pupil textbook, Grade 3) — Bulgaria, Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika), 1st edition 2018, ISBN 978-619-7457-41-4; seven authors headed by Таня Иванова Вълкова; 90,336,271 bytes, 146 PDF pp.; printed folio = PDF page − 1. FULL BOOK (112 lessons, contents to printed p. 140). Pupil textbook.
